# FAQ bank: Torque E-Bikes

Every question appears on exactly one page. Answers are 40–65 words. FAQ rich results were retired by Google in May 2026, so the FAQ markup is for AI answer engines and accessibility, not for rich snippets.

## Homepage (8)
- **Does Torque E-Bikes ship across Australia?** [Navigational]
  Yes. Torque E-Bikes ships Australia-wide from Brisbane. Lithium batteries travel as Class 9 dangerous goods freight, so the team confirms delivery details and cost with you before payment. Browse the shop, add products to your cart and we will confirm freight for your postcode.
- **How do I pay for an order?** [Navigational]
  You can pay by bank transfer, PayID or crypto, and crypto payments receive a 10% discount. After you place an order the team sends payment details, and the order is released for dispatch once cleared funds are received. Orders must reach the $499.99 minimum.
- **What does Torque E-Bikes sell?** [Navigational]
  Torque E-Bikes is a Brisbane-based shop for electric dirt bikes, e-bikes, conversion kits, parts and riding gear from more than 100 brands. Browse the categories for e-mountain bikes, commuter bikes, cargo bikes, kids’ bikes, batteries, brakes, helmets and more. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.
- **Are Torque E-Bikes electric dirt bikes road legal?** [Informational]
  No. Electric dirt bikes and e-bikes above 250W are for off-road and private property use only in Australia. Only pedal-assist e-bikes that meet the 250W standard can be used on public roads. Check your state road authority’s rules before you ride.
- **How do I choose between an e-bike and an electric dirt bike?** [Informational]
  Choose an e-bike for roads and paths with pedal assist, and an electric dirt bike for off-road riding on private land. Think about where you will ride first, then compare power, battery and price within the category that suits you.
- **How can the team help me choose a model?** [Navigational]
  Message the team on WhatsApp on 0480 804 189 with where you ride, your budget and the rider’s height, and they will suggest models from the range. You can also call or use the contact form for a reply. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.
- **Where is Torque E-Bikes based?** [Navigational]
  Torque E-Bikes is based in Brisbane, Queensland, and is a trading name of The Torque Team Pty Ltd (ABN 27 126 064 668). Orders ship Australia-wide, and the Contact page lists phone, WhatsApp and email details. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.
- **Does Torque E-Bikes sell parts and gear as well as bikes?** [Navigational]
  Yes. The range includes batteries, chargers, brakes, wheels, lights, racks, locks, phone mounts, Sur-Ron spares and riding gear such as helmets and gloves. Check each product page for fitment, and ask the team on WhatsApp if you are unsure what suits your bike.

## FAQ page (23)

### About Torque E-Bikes
- **Who is Torque E-Bikes?** [Navigational]
  Torque E-Bikes is the trading name of The Torque Team Pty Ltd (ABN 27 126 064 668), based in Brisbane. The shop sells electric dirt bikes, e-bikes, conversion kits, parts and gear to customers across Australia and ships Australia-wide. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.
- **How do I contact the team?** [Navigational]
  Message or call 0480 804 189, which is also the WhatsApp number, or email sales@torquee-bikesaustralia.com.au. You can also use the contact form, and the team replies with product advice, quotes and order updates. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.
- **How does a wholesale account with Torque E-Bikes work?** [Navigational]
  Workshops, retailers and conversion specialists can request a wholesale account through the Wholesale page. The team checks the business details, including the ABN, and replies with account terms and pricing. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp. Freight is confirmed with you before payment.

### Ordering and payment
- **What payment methods do you accept?** [Navigational]
  You can pay by bank transfer, PayID or crypto. Crypto payments receive a 10% discount. After you place an order, the team sends payment details, and the order is released once cleared funds arrive. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.
- **Is there a minimum order?** [Navigational]
  Yes, the minimum order is $499.99. Add accessories or parts to reach it if a single item falls below the minimum. Prices are in Australian dollars, and the team can confirm your total before you pay. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.
- **How do I place an order?** [Navigational]
  Add products to your cart, check out and submit the order form, or message the team on WhatsApp. The team confirms stock, freight and the payment details with you, and ships the order once payment clears. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.
- **How do I get a quote from the team?** [Navigational]
  Message the team on WhatsApp or use the contact form with the products and your postcode. The team replies with confirmed pricing and freight so you know the full cost before you pay. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.

### Shipping and delivery
- **Do you ship Australia-wide?** [Navigational]
  Yes. We ship Australia-wide from Brisbane. Delivery costs and timeframes depend on the item and your location, and the team confirms them with you before payment so there are no surprises. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp. Freight is confirmed with you before payment.
- **How are lithium batteries shipped?** [Navigational]
  Lithium batteries are Class 9 dangerous goods and travel only with carriers approved for dangerous goods freight. This can affect delivery times and some remote locations, so the team arranges freight with you before you pay. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.
- **What should I do if my order arrives damaged?** [Navigational]
  Check the parcel on arrival. If anything is damaged, faulty or not as ordered, email the team within 48 hours of delivery with your order number and photos, and they will arrange a remedy. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.

### Off-road use and Australian rules
- **Are electric dirt bikes road legal in Australia?** [Informational]
  No. Electric dirt bikes and e-bikes above 250W are for off-road and private property use only, and are not road legal or registrable. Check your state or territory road authority before riding, and wear full protective gear. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.
- **Which e-bikes can I ride on the road?** [Informational]
  Pedal-assist e-bikes that meet the Australian 250W standard can be used on public roads and paths, subject to state rules. Models above 250W are marked off-road only on their product pages. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp. Freight is confirmed with you before payment.
- **Do I need a licence to ride an electric dirt bike?** [Informational]
  Rules depend on where you ride and your state, and electric dirt bikes are limited to private property and off-road areas. Confirm requirements with your state road authority and the land owner before riding. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.
- **Can children ride electric dirt bikes?** [Informational]
  Many kids’ models exist, but suitability depends on the child’s size and ability and the manufacturer’s guidance. Always supervise, use a fitted helmet and protective gear, and ride only on private property or approved off-road areas. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.

### Batteries and safety
- **How should I charge and store an e-bike battery?** [Informational]
  Use the charger supplied for your battery, charge in a ventilated spot and never leave it charging unattended. Store it in a cool, dry place away from flammable items, partly charged if it will sit for weeks. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.
- **How long does an e-bike battery last?** [Informational]
  Battery life depends on use, charging habits and temperature. Each charge gives a range that varies with rider weight, terrain and assist level, and the pack gradually loses capacity over its life. Check the manufacturer’s guidance for your model. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.
- **Which spare batteries and chargers does Torque E-Bikes list?** [Navigational]
  The batteries, chargers and electronics category lists packs, chargers, controllers and wiring harnesses for several bikes. Check the product page for the models each part fits, or ask the team on WhatsApp. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp. Freight is confirmed with you before payment.

### Kids and beginners
- **What is a good first electric bike for a child?** [Informational]
  Start with a bike sized for your child, with a speed limiter or power modes and good brakes. An electric balance bike suits younger children, while mini dirt bikes suit older, more confident riders. Always supervise and use protective gear.
- **Do I need protective gear for e-bikes?** [Informational]
  A certified helmet is essential, and gloves help too. For off-road electric dirt bikes add goggles, boots and body armour. The helmets, gloves and protection category lists gear in adult and kids’ sizes. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.

### Parts, fitment and warranty
- **How do I know a part fits my bike?** [Navigational]
  Check the models listed on the product page, then confirm with the team if you are unsure. Message your bike’s make, model and year on WhatsApp and they will check fitment before you order. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.
- **Do products come with a warranty?** [Navigational]
  Manufacturer warranties apply and vary by brand and product. Australian Consumer Law guarantees also apply and cannot be excluded. Contact the team with your order number if something fails, and they will explain the next steps. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.
- **Can I return an item if I change my mind?** [Navigational]
  Contact the team before sending anything back. Approval, return freight and any restocking costs are agreed in advance, and batteries and used items may not be eligible. Your consumer guarantee rights for faulty goods are not affected. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.
- **Which Sur-Ron and Talaria parts does Torque E-Bikes list?** [Navigational]
  The Sur-Ron spares category lists swingarms, wheels, charging parts and more, and other parts lists name the models they fit. If your model is not listed, message the team and they will check what is available. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.

### /shop/electric-dirt-bikes/
- **What electric dirt bikes does Torque E-Bikes list?** [Navigational]
  Torque E-Bikes currently lists 49 electric dirt bikes, priced from $949 to $26,849 with a typical price near $5,699. - **Does Torque E-Bikes ship electric dirt bikes across Australia?** [Navigational]
  Yes. We ship electric dirt bikes Australia-wide from Brisbane, and the team confirms the freight cost and delivery timing with you before payment. Lithium batteries ship as Class 9 dangerous goods freight. Message the team on WhatsApp with your postcode and the products you want for a firm quote.
- **Which electric dirt bikes brands does Torque E-Bikes carry?** [Navigational]
  The range includes Talaria (7), Sur-Ron (5), 79Bike (3), Electro & Co (3), with the number of products listed in brackets. Open a category page to see every brand side by side, then open a product page for the specification and current price in Australian dollars.
- **What should I compare when choosing electric dirt bikes?** [Informational]
  Compare motor power, battery capacity, weight, suspension travel and rider height. Put two or three models side by side on their product pages, check that each specification matches how and where you will use it, and ask the team on WhatsApp if you want help narrowing the list.
- **What changes the price of electric dirt bikes?** [Informational]
  Prices here run from $949 to $26,849. Lower-priced options suit lighter or occasional use, while higher-priced ones usually add stronger components, capacity or support. Match the specification to your use and avoid paying for features you will not use. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.
- **What are the rules for using electric dirt bikes in Australia?** [Informational]
  Electric dirt bikes are for off-road and private property use only in Australia, and they are not road legal or registrable. Check your state or territory road authority and the land owner’s rules before riding, and wear a helmet and protective gear.

- **What type of electric bike suits which rider?** [Informational]

- **How does an e-bike differ from a normal bike?** [Informational]
  An e-bike makes hills and longer rides easier and widens the trips you can do by bike. A normal bike is lighter and cheaper to own. If distance or hills put you off riding, an e-bike usually pays for itself in use.
- **How do I compare e-bikes fairly?** [Informational]
  Compare bikes of the same type, then check motor, battery capacity, brakes, frame size and weight. Look at warranty support and spare-part availability as well, and compare prices within the same category rather than across different types of bike. The team can confirm details on WhatsApp.

- **What should I not compromise on with a cheap e-bike?** [Informational]
